## Operation status handover / archival rule

**While an operation is active**, `PROJECT-STATUS.md` displays the full discovered-source/serializer register for that operation, with each newly found source path logged **before** implementation and test updates recorded as they occur. New discoveries are allowed and must appear with a precise status immediately.

**When O06 is truly ACCEPTED** (all G1–G6 accepted with source-backed evidence and DEV/PRD verification): preserve the complete O06 source register, status history, test journals and logs as permanent repository records. Before removing the expanded O06 section from this page, archive its final Markdown snapshot under `catalog/compliance/archive/UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.md` and retain the canonical JSON `UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.json` without deleting records. Replace the entire O06 live register on this page with **one compact O06 ACCEPTED row**, including final commit, acceptance date and links to the archived source inventory and verification evidence. **Never mark O06 complete or hide unresolved statuses before acceptance.** Disable O06's automatic injection into this page once archived; further O06 regression results belong in its archival/evidence files unless they cause reopening.

Then reserve the live detail area for **O07**, using a separate O07 register and new discoveries, with the same update-before-work/test-by-test discipline. Repeat for subsequent operations. This page remains an active-operation dashboard; the detailed source/test archive remains accessible in Git.

## O06 live source serializer register

**Canonical recovery reference:** this table is updated during discovery, mapping, implementation, tests and deployment, not retrospectively.

Found source entries: **292** (R82=26, R83=26, R96=26, U86=49, U97=49, D97=7, C98=50, C227=5, T99=54).

**Current per-entry status:** EXCLUDED=38, MAPPED=57, PARTIAL=197.

**States:** `DISCOVERED` = located in Epic source only; `MAPPED` = source behavior/reader mapped; `PARTIAL` = a subset of the source-defined serializer is implemented/tested, but remaining source-branch verification or code fixes are unresolved; `GAP` = confirmed missing implementation; `BLOCKED` = named prerequisite; `EXCLUDED` = proven non-saveable or runtime-only; `DEV_PASS`/`PRD_PASS` = explicit source-scoped named tests passed at that target; `ACCEPTED` = source contract signed off. An entry that has passed a narrow fixture but may have unresolved fields remains PARTIAL.

**Mandatory live workflow:** (1) On discovering a symbol, add it immediately as DISCOVERED with source location. (2) Update to MAPPED/PARTIAL/GAP when the reader/code is inspected, naming remaining work. (3) Record test script, evidence and exact outcome when run; change the same row before starting another task. (4) Commit/push/deploy the updated status with each bounded checkpoint. (5) Never turn a registration or a corpus-only result into serializer acceptance. A latest DEV/PRD PASS means only that the named fixture passed there; it NEVER means class acceptance while the status is PARTIAL. Check the Outstanding / evidence column for the precise unfinished work. Some PARTIAL entries need only verification; others still need code. When the complete source contract is demonstrated, promote that row explicitly to ACCEPTED with evidence. Never replace an older failure without retaining its journal. The O06 test runner records STARTED then PASS/FAIL here automatically, while leaving class-level acceptance unchanged. The six O06 gates remain fixed even if new rows are discovered.

Machine-readable file: `catalog/compliance/UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.json`. Regenerate/update using `python catalog/bin/sync-ue1-o06-serializers.py` or `--set-status ID STATUS --reader ... --test ... --evidence ... --remaining ...`. Existing statuses survive rescans. Paths below are relative to their corresponding Epic source roots. **These rows are source sightings, not all distinct on-disk requirements.**

### O06 source-version expansion using local Graphify (2026-10-22)

**Discovered and registered immediately:** after indexing other UE1 versions, the live O06 register was extended with **106 source-version sightings**: v0.82 26 registrations (`R82`), v0.83 26 (`R83`), v0.86x 49 (`U86`), and v1.227 5 explicit `Serialize` definitions (`C227`). All 106 entries were recorded first as **DISCOVERED** with local Epic source path/line; 52 early-v9 sightings (R82/R83) were subsequently advanced to **MAPPED** after a reproducible byte-identity proof across 200/200 matching C/C++ source and header files (`tools/graphify/Compare-1996-Versions.py`, [evidence](evidence/o06/20261022-ue1-v9-v082-v083-source-identity.txt)). Both trees are the exact same code for the examined 200 files, so do not treat those sightings as 52 separate code implementation gaps. Their v9 UnrealDB field/reader parity still requires G1 reconciliation. The remaining 54 newer source sightings retain **DISCOVERED** status. Original 172 entries and recorded tests were preserved. The new rows are version-specific comparison work for G1/G2/G3/G4.

**Version facts checked against source:** v0.82 and v0.83 `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009`, v0.84a `0x000A`; v0.86x and v0.867 `UNREAL_FILE_VERSION=23`. Equal numeric versions do **not** prove that every serialized subclass layout stayed identical: compare actual native methods, headers and changed structures before mapping the new rows. The v1.227 source tree's provenance is not yet established, so `C227` sightings are discovery-only. No O06 completion gate was added, closed or falsely promoted.

### O06 fixed closure board — source census and remaining gates (2026-10-22)

**Purpose:** Stop open-ended serializer-by-serializer work. This board is the authoritative O06 progress tracker. Older narrative paragraphs describe historical checkpoints, **not** today's remaining-work list. O06 cannot be called complete merely because the currently installed texture/sound/font corpus passes.

**Observed source census (DEV local Epic C++):** v0.84a registers **26** `AUTOREGISTER_RESOURCE` classes; `UCamera`, `UPlayer`, editor `UTransBuffer` are confirmed non-saveable, leaving **23 source-saveable registrations** to reconcile. v0.867 registers **49 unique C++ object classes**; most share inherited `SerializeHeader`/`StandardSerializeData` and some are transient, so 49 is *not* 49 separate on-disk serializers. UT99 v400 source has **49 explicit `::Serialize(FArchive&)` C++ definitions** (including runtime/editor/inherited behaviours); each must be classified, but not every one is a valid disk requirement.

**Important coverage limitation:** `CatalogUE1ClassicNativeObjectReader` handles 11 explicit native object classes and now dispatches 13 source-typed UProperty subclasses through `CatalogUE1ClassicPropertyNativeReader`; every new C98/T99 class/field status is PARTIAL until its entire source contract is proven, returning `class_specific_serializer_unimplemented` for other class-native tails. `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader` now dispatches `classIndex=0` to `CatalogUE1ClassicClassMetadataReader`: source-typed no-script/empty-default metadata is decoded, while nonempty script/defaults and StateFrame remain explicitly unimplemented. The 92/92 and 2,465/2,465 results are genuine but cover a narrow pinned corpus; they **do not** establish full UE1 class/payload support. This is a confirmed implementation gap, not an audit/documentation problem.

| Gate | Exactly what must be done for source-defined O06 | Current state / bounded exit |
|---|---|---|
| **G1 — original 1996 v9/v10** | Reconcile all **23 saveable registrations** to the already implemented native readers, data layouts and typed actor/class property handling; test any actually missing source-defined field/branch. Explicitly separate unused native padding and runtime pointer linking. | **G1 SOURCE-MAPPING ACCEPTED (1/6 fixed O06 gates)**: all 26 Epic v0.84a registrations exactly match source; 23/23 saveable classes have source-typed reader + named tests and byte-layout staging coverage, three source-confirmed unsaveable classes excluded. All 26 source-family named verifiers PASS independently on DEV and PRD, and the deterministic `verify-ue1-o06-g1-source-coverage.php` acceptance driver passes 291/291 source/reader/current test-hash/journal/deployment checks on both. Remains PARTIAL for genuine-package parity G5 and runtime-linking O10, not falsely marked entire O06 accepted. |
| **G2 — original 1997 v22/v23** | Classify all **49 registered classes** into file-serializable, inherited/shared, and transient; reconcile *distinct* `SerializeHeader`, database record, `SerializeData`, `SerializeBin` and bytecode cases to existing readers and tests. Fix only demonstrated missing branches. `UTransBuffer` source explicitly says it is not saveable. | **PARTIAL, G2 OPEN:** 49/49 v0.867 U97 registrations now classified (39 existing source-typed reader mappings, 4 actor inheritance mappings, 6 source-confirmed transient exclusions including UCamera/UTransBuffer); same actor/linker source families classified in May 1997 v0.86x (16 additional sightings). Six affected shared-class/parent-schema/metadata named tests pass DEV 6/6 and PRD 6/6, source-classification verifier 46/46. Remaining G2: **49/49 original May v0.86x source registrations classified: 43 PARTIAL, 6 EXCLUDED, 0 DISCOVERED and 0 GAP**. All 30 formerly unclassified May registrations now name the Epic May and corresponding July declaration, existing production reader, named test, explicit outstanding May source-contract/provenance check; their 11 distinct named fixtures PASS **DEV 11/11 and PRD 11/11**. This is class-to-reader reconciliation, NOT full May on-disk parity; May-versus-July same-version-23 CPF_Intrinsic byte gate corrected in both production binary-property readers (25/25 inheritance fixtures, now 42/42 external native UProperties fixtures). UProperties now infers the source only if one complete fixed-width May/July archive matches the data length exactly (affected DEV 6/6, PRD 6/6); actor headers and ambiguous/variable-width data still require explicit lineage, never silently skipping a 0x1000 field. A third same-version `UTexture` header discrepancy is fixed: May persists Palette only and FrictionC before ReflectivityC (122B); July adds 4 texture references and flips those coefficients (138B). Native length now selects May/July automatically at exactly 122/138B, or accepts explicit lineage; texture 45/45 checks and DEV 7/7 / PRD 7/7 affected suite PASS. **0 additional U86 sightings DISCOVERED** (30 now mapped as PARTIAL, not accepted); the three previously confirmed May gaps are resolved to PARTIAL with named tests: `UPrimitive` native bound (41B), `UBounds` wire-identical 25B and `UBspNodes` May 55B / July 56B, while `UBspSurfs` May wire identity is now mapped. All still await genuine package/oracle proof. `UMesh`/`UModel` direct source-accurate serialization now implements both May/July (shared BYTE-bound and WORD-vs-INT rotation; 44/44 mesh and 40/40 model checks), DEV/PRD 6/6 affected named tests PASS. Wider v22/v23 class/source parity and genuine-file matching remain open; no source-wide acceptance yet. |

**G4 `UTextBuffer` progress (2026-10-22):** Implemented Epic v1.200/UT99 v400 `UTextBuffer::Serialize` native tail (signed Pos/Top and ANSI / source version-gated UTF-16LE FString) in `CatalogUE1ClassicNativeObjectReader.php`. **13/13 synthetic UE1-package positive and negative checks PASS**, including missing terminator, invalid UTF-16, overflow, and legacy v50 StateName/GroupName framing. Affected genuine **92 packages / 2,465 tagged exports / 15,475 tags** remain PASS; native negative regression PASS. All three named tests **DEV 3/3 and PRD 3/3 journaled PASS** with output, code hashes and START/FINISH events. C98 and T99 `UTextBuffer::Serialize` register entries were changed **GAP → PARTIAL** (remaining genuine TextBuffer samples/source branch verification explicitly unresolved); they are **not** class-wide ACCEPTED and G4 remains OPEN. No O07 bulk decoding or O10 runtime claims.

**G3/G4 `UEnum` progress (2026-10-22):** Implemented the explicit Epic v1.200 and UT99 v400 serialized `UField::Serialize` (SuperField and Next package references) and `UEnum::Serialize` (`TArray<FName>` compact count/index values), with strict bounds and source-defined names, preserving unresolved references rather than pretending runtime linking. **13/13 synthetic UE1-package positive/negative cases PASS** (v50/v69, invalid object/name refs, truncated arrays/counts, inherited UObject framing, extension). Combined affected verification **DEV 4/4, PRD 4/4 named tests PASS** with 92 genuine package corpus unchanged. C98/T99 `UEnum::Serialize` advanced from GAP to PARTIAL, not ACCEPTED; full class/parent/version/source census remains open.

**G3 `UConst` progress (2026-10-22):** Implemented Epic v1.200/UT99 v400 `UConst::Serialize` (inherited UField SuperField/Next references followed by source-gated ANSI or UTF-16LE `FString Value`), with strict bounds, terminating-NUL enforcement, invalid reference/count/UTF16 rejection and opaque trailing-extension identification. New 15/15 native fixture checks PASS, together with other new class-native readers and existing genuine 92-package and malformed-payload tests: **DEV 5/5, PRD 5/5 named tests journaled PASS**. C98 and T99 `UConst::Serialize` GAP→PARTIAL; full classIndex=0 default/class metadata and untested genuine UConst corpus still open. All six O06 gates remain OPEN.

**G3 classic UProperty family implementation (2026-10-22):** Added `CatalogUE1ClassicPropertyNativeReader.php` and native dispatch for 13 property kinds. Reconciled Epic v1.200 `UnProp.cpp` and UT99 v400: `UField` SuperField/Next, `INT ArrayDim`, `DWORD PropertyFlags`, `FName Category`, conditional `_WORD RepOffset` when `CPF_Net=0x20`, UT99 <=61 old-flag cleanup, then subclass-specific source-ordered Enum / PropertyClass / MetaClass / StringSize / Struct / Inner / Count / Key/Value fields. ## Mandatory source-first execution and acceptance discipline (2026-10-21)

**Policy correction after repeated source-coverage misses and O01 scope expansion:**

1. **Inventory source before interpreting or editing a format.** For each engine, recursively enumerate ALL supplied engine/game source roots and relevant C++ load/save code; search format tags, headers AND EOF trailers, version gates, serializers, loader functions, and all source revisions. Register the exact source file, symbol/line and branch difference. The absent `UnObjVer.h` is NOT evidence of absent source: original Unreal v0.82–0.84a use `UnRes.cpp` and trailer metadata, not the later `ULinker`.
2. **No unsupported "source gap" assumptions.** Before calling information unavailable, search alternative file names, legacy subsystems, adjacent versions, engine and game trees, actual loader/writer implementations, and compiled artifacts where useful; record searches and distinguish (a) source genuinely absent for a particular fork, (b) UnrealDB reader not yet built, and (c) independent test fixture not yet available. Treat source definitions as implementation authority; do not delay source-driven work pending an optional old executable.
3. **Freeze a narrow operation contract BEFORE coding.** O01 is file-format identification, version/licensee extraction, selected-game dispatch and honest unsupported/invalid results. Full name/import/export decoding is O02–O04; dependencies O05; payloads O06/O07; production SQL/search O09; complete workflow acceptance O10. Later operation failures must not block earlier acceptance unless they actually change its inputs or outputs.
4. **Work each operation in one bounded source→diff→fix→accept sequence:** exhaustive source mapping once, real production caller coverage, minimal correction, source-anchored positive/negative tests, standard regressions, evidence of observed differences, Git commit/push and immediate PRD SHA-256 deployment. Never add arbitrary B-number cycles or new acceptance conditions after work begins without a demonstrated scope error.
5. **Publish measurable results:** per-operation PASS/FAIL and exact open acceptance checks; genuine corpus and regression counts separately; source findings with true status (source present, code absent, or test missing). Do not conflate tests passed with engine accepted. After closing O01, **advance to O02** without reopening O01 for unrelated improvements.

The source-first rule is mandatory for UE1 and repeats unchanged for UE2/2.5, UE3/UDK, UE4 and UE5. Review all supplied code branches before stating that any engine information is missing.

## UE1 source branch matrix — discovered, not assumed equivalent

| Source tree | Mechanically observed file version / minimum | Distinction and remaining work |
|---|---|---|
| `Unreal [v0.82]` | `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009` | Original EOF resource trailer, source-defined names/imports/exports/native headers and unverified staging implemented. **Type-specific object payloads O06 still open** |
| `Unreal [v0.83]` | `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009` | Same source-defined v9 family structurally read and staged; true loaded ResArray runtime binding unavailable, recorded as not-evaluated; payloads O06 |
| `Unreal [v0.84a]` | `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x000A` | Original v10 structural tables/headers read and staged; type/name source lookup projected, payloads O06 |
| `Unreal [v0.86x]` | `UNREAL_FILE_VERSION=23`, min 22 | Early 32-byte `Unrealfile\x1A` header; separate format |
| `Unreal [v0.867]` | Version 23, min 22 | `FUnrealfileSummary`, not modern UE1 package summary |
| `Unreal [v1.200]` | `PACKAGE_FILE_VERSION=61`, min 34 | Classic numeric tag; different pre-v68 summary serializer; original complete source |
| `Unreal [v1.224]` | Version 68, min 60 | Tree labelled INCOMPLETE; provenance and source gaps |
| `Unreal [v1.227]` | Version 69, min 60 | Source includes licencee/Legend changes; header/linker bodies incomplete; git HEAD previously `2bd1ce9` |
| `UT99 [v1.400]` | Version 68, min 60 | Complete matching C++ `ULinkerLoad` source inspected |
| `UT99 v432` | Version 69, min 60 | Folder labelled v432 but `ENGINE_VERSION=430` in header; pin actual source config; git HEAD previously `db43a2e` |
| `Engine\UE1` | Version 61, min 34 | Community-maintained version-200-derived baseline; git HEAD previously `403fe5e` |

All paths above are under `L:\Source\Games\Unreal`, `L:\Source\Games\UT99`, or `L:\Source\Engine\UE1`. Each relevant fork must be traced under its correct file-format family. UE2 and newer game roots are reserved for later phases.

**Source coverage clarification (2026-10-21):** Source-code absence is **not** a blocker for the early v0.82/0.83/0.84a resource formats or v0.86x/v0.867 Unrealfile format. Their structural/native metadata readers and typed unverified staging are now implemented, but complete object/property/payload readers remain O06 and onward. The v0.82/0.83 resource versions are **9** and v0.84a is **10**, distinct from later classic package versions 35–69. Unreal v1.224 directory genuinely contains mostly headers (8 C++ files observed); v1.227 contains the file summary/layout headers, while some specific linker implementations are absent. Nearby complete source branches can inform shared behaviour but must not automatically be treated as identical. Genuine old-format binary fixtures and native runtime runs improve *independent validation*; **neither is a prerequisite to read/implement the available documented source**. The remaining production MySQL/search work is an **UnrealDB implementation test**, not an Epic-source gap. See `catalog/compliance/UE1-IMPLEMENTATION.md`.

| UE1-D005: production header-only helper returned fallback blank headers after reader failures | CORRECTED for UE1 only | `catalog/lib/CatalogParser.php` | New 4-check real-entry-point test (three failed before fix, 4/4 pass after); 15/15 selected regressions passed |
| UE1-D006: pre-68 inspector assumed heritage GUID always inline at byte 44 | CORRECTED; reader already source-aligned | `catalog/src/Application/Catalog/CatalogPackageHeaderInspector.php` | 4 of 5 source-anchored variant checks failed before fix; 5/5 pass after; genuine 61/61 corpus and 15/15 regression scripts pass |
| UE1-D007: preliminary UE1 game admission still accepted UE2-only Killing Floor tag | CORRECTED in B05; UE2 still admitted | `catalog/lib/GameProfiles.php::gp_classify_file()` | Real profile/classifier/V5 test reproduced failure; two negative path checks pass after fix; 92/92 genuine source/V5 disk roundtrips and 19/19 selected scripts pass |

The early `Unrealfile` header now returns an explicit **unsupported format** result with UE1 identity/version at classification. It is **not** deserialized or accepted as a usable package. This is a genuine unresolved feature gap.

**Genuine legacy corpus discovered 2026-10-21:** `L:\Source\Engine\UE1\Source\Unreal\Textures` contains 61 standard-magic `.utx` packages: 59 are versions 35–60, and two are version 61; all licensee versions are zero. The other 85 files are PCX images. Per-file SHA-256/version inventory at `catalog/compliance/inventory/ue1-textures-v35-v61.csv`, explanation at `catalog/compliance/inventory/UE1-TEXTURES-CORPUS.md`. **B04 VERIFIED (limited):** all 61/61 SHA-verified files parse via the canonical UE1 reader without reported issues, including matching name/import/export counts and actual production header helper; an independent direct-byte source-defined 44-byte summary read matches all 11 serialized header fields in 61/61 files, and file-examine GUIDs agree in 61/61. These files all have heritageCount=1, heritageOffset=44; they do **not** cover other heritage arrangements. Original compiled Epic engine/runtime comparison and full workflow acceptance remain OPEN.

**B05 verified (bounded):** new installed genuine UE1 v68/v69 corpus 31/31; aggregate genuine-file profile/reader/source-specific V5 container write/read 92/92, including version69/licensee227. The UE1 preliminary classifier no longer admits the UE2-only Killing Floor tag (UE1-D007); UE2-only tag remains admitted under UE2. Two negative controls passed, 19/19 selected regression scripts passed on DEV, and six relevant executable checks passed on deployed PRD. Actual production MySQL publishing and search, early string-tagged binary reader, missing source fork coverage and native Epic executable parity remain **OPEN**.

B02 checks passed: `verify-ue1-early-format-identification.php` **9/9**, plus **12/12** selected UE1/UE2/UE4/UE5 regression scripts, PHP syntax and source-anchored tests. B03 checked the live header-only helper: 4/4 assertions passed after three baseline failures, plus **15/15** selected regressions. Synthetic fixtures isolate branch decisions; native engine and genuine-package corpus equivalence are **not** established. The `.u` files sampled in the v0.86x/v0.867 source directories are script text, not binary package corpus.
